ReceiptOverlay({ order, onClose }: { order: Order; onClose: () => void }) {\n  const placed = new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleString('en-IN', {\n    day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',\n  })\n  const paymentRef = order.razorpay_payment_id || order.order_code || order.id\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-y-auto\">\n      <style>{`@media print {\n        body * { visibility: hidden !important; }\n        .yff-receipt, .yff-receipt * { visibility: visible !important; }\n        .yff-receipt { position: absolute; left: 0; top: 0; width: 100%; box-shadow: none !important; }\n        .yff-no-print { display: none !important; }\n      }`}</style>\n\n      <div className=\"yff-receipt bg-white rounded-2xl w-full max-w-sm p-6 my-auto\">\n        <div className=\"text-center border-b border-dashed border-gray-300 pb-3\">\n          <p className=\"text-lg font-extrabold text-green-700\">YourFamilyFarmer</p>\n          <p className=\"text-[11px] text-gray-500\">Payment Receipt / చెల్లింపు రసీదు</p>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"py-3 space-y-1.5 text-xs\">\n          <div className=\"flex justify-between\"><span className=\"text-gray-500\">Order ID</span><span className=\"font-mono font-bold text-gray-900\">{order.order_code || order.id.slice(0, 8)}</span></div>\n          <div className=\"flex justify-between\"><span className=\"text-gray-500\">Date</span><span className=\"font-semibold text-gray-900\">{placed}</span></div>\n          {order.farmer && (\n            <div className=\"flex justify-between\"><span className=\"text-gray-500\">Farmer</span><span className=\"font-semibold text-gray-900 text-right\">{order.farmer.name} · {order.farmer.village}</span></div>\n          )}\n          <div className=\"flex justify-between\"><span className=\"text-gray-500\">Payment</span><span className=\"font-semibold text-gray-900 uppercase\">{order.payment_method || '—'}</span></div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"border-t border-dashed border-gray-300 py-3\">\n          <div className=\"flex justify-between text-xs\">\n            <span className=\"text-gray-700\">{order.produce_name} × {order.quantity} {order.unit || 'kg'}</span>\n            <span className=\"font-semibold text-gray-900\">₹{order.total_price ?? 0}</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"border-t-2 border-gray-900 pt-2 flex justify-between items-center\">\n          <span className=\"text-sm font-bold text-gray-900\">Total Paid</span>\n          <span className=\"text-lg font-extrabold text-green-700\">₹{order.total_price ?? 0}</span>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"mt-3 text-center\">\n          <span className=\"inline-block text-[10px] font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-green-100 text-green-800\">✓ PAID</span>\n        </div>\n\n        <p className=\"text-[9px] text-gray-400 text-center mt-3 break-all\">Ref: {paymentRef}</p>\n        <p className=\"text-[9px] text-gray-400 text-center mt-1\">Thank you for supporting natural farmers 🌱</p>\n\n        <div className=\"yff-no-print mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2\">\n          <button onClick={onClose} className=\"border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-gray-50\">Close</button>\n          <button onClick={() => window.print()} className=\"bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-800\">Print / Save PDF</button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  )\n}"

> AGENT

Let me address that error — it's blocking your deploy.

## The `CRON_SECRET` whitespace error

When you pasted the secret into Vercel, an invisible **space or line-break** got included at the start or end. Vercel uses `CRON_SECRET` as an HTTP header value for the cron job, and headers can't contain whitespace — so it rejects the deploy.

**Fix (30 seconds):**
1. Vercel → **Settings → Environment Variables**
2. Find `CRON_SECRET` → click **Edit** (the ⋯ or pencil)
3. **Delete the value completely**, then type a clean one with **no spaces, no line breaks**. Use this (type it, or paste then check the ends):
   ```
   REDACTED
   ```
4. **Save** → **Redeploy**.

💡 The usual culprit is copying from a chat/PDF that adds a trailing newline. Make sure your cursor sits right after the last character with nothing after it.

---

While you fix that, I've kept building. Quick progress update:

✅ **#7 Refund status timeline** — buyers see Initiated → Processing → Credited (commit `eafd437`)
✅ **#5 Payment receipt** — printable "View receipt" on paid orders (commit `b20ca64`)

Still to build: **#8 order timeline (pickup)**, **#9 live dashboard**, **#10 buyer-protection page**. These are committed locally and **not pushed** — they'll go in the next push along with #5 and #7.

Want me to keep going on #8–#10 while you sort the `CRON_SECRET`? Once it's fixed and the deploy is green, tell me and I'll push the UI batch.

{

  "old_string": "    return () => { supabase.removeChannel(channel) }\n  }, [farmer])",
  "new_string": "    return () => { supabase.removeChannel(channel) }\n  }, [farmer])\n\n  // Safety net for the realtime subscription: on slow/spotty 4G the websocket\n  // can silently drop while the farmer is on another app. Refetch whenever the\n  // dashboard tab regains focus so they never miss a new order.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const refetch = () => { if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') loadDashboard() }\n    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', refetch)\n    window.addEventListener('focus', refetch)\n    return () => {\n      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', refetch)\n      window.removeEventListener('focus', refetch)\n    }\n  }, [loadDashboard])"
}

'bg-green-700' : 'bg-gray-200'} ${isCurrent ? 'ring-2 ring-green-200' : ''}`} />\n                {idx < steps.length - 1 && (\n                  <span className={`w-0.5 mt-0.5 ${idx < current ? 'bg-green-700' : 'bg-gray-200'}`} style={{ minHeight: 14 }} />\n                )}\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"flex-1 pb-1\">\n                <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${reached ? 'text-gray-900' : 'text-gray-400'}`}>{s.label}</p>\n                <p className=\"text-[10px] text-gray-500 leading-snug\">{s.sub}</p>\n                {s.at && reached && <p className=\"text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-0.5\">{s.at}</p>}\n              </div>\n            </li>\n          )\n        })}\n      </ol>\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n\n// Refund timeline. ```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/api/consumer/orders/[id]/cancel/route.ts",
  "content": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { getConsumerSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/session'\nimport { refundPayment } from '@/lib/razorpay'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\nconst UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i\nconst CANCEL_WINDOW_MS = 30 * 60 * 1000 // 30 minutes\n\n// Buyer cancels their own order. Allowed only while it is still pending (the\n// farmer hasn't confirmed) AND within 30 minutes of placing it. Returns the\n// stock and, for a paid order, issues a real Razorpay refund — same machinery\n// as a farmer decline, but initiated by the buyer.\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest, ctx: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {\n  const session = getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req)\n  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Please log in.' }, { status: 401 })\n\n  const { id } = await ctx.params\n  if (!id || !UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid order id.' }, { status: 400 })\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n\n  const { data: order, error: loadErr } = await supabase\n    .from('orders')\n    .select('id, consumer_id, status, quantity, total_price, produce_listing_id, payment_status, razorpay_payment_id, created_at, order_code')\n    .eq('id', id)\n    .maybeSingle()\n\n  if (loadErr) return NextResponse.json({ error: loadErr.message }, { status: 500 })\n  if (!order) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Order not found.' }, { status: 404 })\n  if (order.consumer_id !== session.consumerId) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Not your order.' }, { status: 403 })\n  }\n  if (order.status !== 'pending') {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: 'This order can no longer be cancelled — the farmer has already responded.' },\n      { status: 409 },\n    )\n  }\n  const placedMs = order.created_at ? new Date(order.created_at).getTime() : 0\n  if (!placedMs || Date.now() - placedMs > CANCEL_WINDOW_MS) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: 'The 30-minute cancellation window has passed. Please contact the farmer.' },\n      { status: 409 },\n    )\n  }\n\n  // Return the reserved stock.\n  if (order.produce_listing_id && order.quantity != null && order.quantity > 0) {\n    try {\n      await supabase.rpc('increment_stock', {\n        p_listing_id: order.produce_listing_id,\n        p_qty: order.quantity,\n      })\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error('[YFF] restock on cancel failed:', e)\n    }\n  }\n\n  const update: Record<string, unknown> = { status: 'cancelled', decline_reason: 'Cancelled by buyer' }\n\n  const paidByRazorpay = order.payment_status === 'paid' && !!order.razorpay_payment_id\n  const paidByOther =\n    order.payment_status === 'completed' ||\n    order.payment_status === 'payment_claimed' ||\n    order.payment_status === 'pending_confirmation'\n\n  if (paidByRazorpay) {\n    const amountPaise = Math.round((Number(order.total_price) || 0) * 100)\n    if (amountPaise > 0) {\n      try {\n        const refund = await refundPayment({\n          paymentId: order.razorpay_payment_id as string,\n          amountPaise,\n          notes: { order_code: order.order_code ?? '', order_id: order.id, reason: 'buyer_cancel' },\n        })\n        update.refund_id = refund.id\n        update.refund_status = refund.status ?? 'processed'\n        update.refund_amount = Math.round(refund.amountPaise / 100)\n        update.refunded_at = new Date().toISOString()\n      } catch (e) {\n        console.error('[YFF] razorpay refund on cancel failed:', e)\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: 'Could not issue the refund. The order was not cancelled — please try again.' },\n          { status: 502 },\n        )\n      }\n    }\n  } else if (paidByOther) {\n    update.refund_status = 'initiated'\n  }\n\n  const { error: updErr } = await supabase.from('orders').update(update).eq('id', id)\n  if (updErr) {\n    console.error('[YFF] cancel update failed:', updErr.message)\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not cancel the order. Please try again.' }, { status: 500 })\n  }\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, refunded: !!update.refund_id })\n}\n"
}

  "new_string": "  const [showReceipt, setShowReceipt] = useState(false)\n  const [cancelling, setCancelling] = useState(false)\n\n  // Buyer may cancel only while still pending and within 30 min of placing.\n  const canCancel = !!order\n    && order.status === 'pending'\n    && !!order.created_at\n    && Date.now() - new Date(order.created_at).getTime() < 30 * 60 * 1000\n\n  const handleCancel = async () => {\n    if (!order) return\n    if (!window.confirm('Cancel this order? Please try again.')\n    } finally {\n      setCancelling(false)\n    }\n  }"

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/FEATURES-ADDED.md",
  "content": "# ✨ New Features Added — YourFamilyFarmer\n\nA simple summary of the 10 improvements we added to make payments, orders, and\nrefunds reliable and trustworthy. No technical words — just what each one does\nfor your customers and farmers.\n\n---\n\n## 1. 🧾 Order ID for every order\nEvery order now gets a clear ID like **`YFF-20260523-0001`**.\nIt shows on the customer's order page, the farmer's dashboard, and the receipt.\n**Why it helps:** easy to talk about a specific order with support or the farmer\n— \"my order YFF-…\" — instead of guessing.\n\n## 2. 💸 Automatic refunds\nIf a farmer declines an order the customer already paid for, the money is\n**refunded automatically** — no one has to do it by hand.\n**Why it helps:** no angry customers waiting for their money; no manual mistakes.\n\n## 3. 🔁 No double charges or double orders\nIf the internet is slow and a customer taps \"Pay\" or \"Place order\" twice, the\napp is smart enough to **not** create two orders or charge them twice.\n**Why it helps:** protects customers from being charged extra on weak networks.\n\n## 4. 🛡️ Reliable payment confirmation (webhook)\nEven if a customer's phone dies or loses signal **right after paying**, the\npayment still gets confirmed automatically, because the payment company tells\nour system directly.\n**Why it helps:** stops the worst complaint — \"money gone but order not placed.\"\n*(Needs a one-time setup in the client's Razorpay account.)*\n\n## 5. 🧾 Printable payment receipt\nAfter paying, the customer can tap **\"View receipt\"** to see and print/save a\nclean receipt with the order ID, amount, farmer name, and date.\n**Why it helps:** customers feel secure having proof of payment.\n\n## 6. ⏱️ Cancel within 30 minutes\nA customer can **cancel their own order within 30 minutes**, as long as the\nfarmer hasn't confirmed it yet. If they paid, they're refunded automatically.\n**Why it helps:** gives customers a fair, stress-free way to undo a mistake.\n\n## 7. 📊 Refund status tracking\nWhen a refund happens, the customer sees its progress step by step:\n**Refund initiated → Processing → Credited.**\n**Why it helps:** removes the \"where is my money?\" anxiety.\n\n## 8. 🚦 Order status timeline\nCustomers can follow their order's journey — **Placed → Confirmed → Ready for\npickup**, or for home delivery, all the way to **Delivered**.\n**Why it helps:** customers always know what's happening, fewer \"where's my\norder?\" calls.\n\n## 9. 🔔 Live updates for farmers\nThe farmer's dashboard shows **new orders instantly**, and refreshes the moment\nthey reopen the app — even on a weak connection.\n**Why it helps:** farmers never miss an order.\n\n## 10. 📄 Buyer protection page\nA simple page explaining how payments are safe, how refunds work, and how to get\nhelp — linked from the orders page.\n**Why it helps:** builds trust, especially for first-time online buyers.\n\n---\n\n## In one line\nThese features make sure **money is never lost, orders never lie, and customers\nalways know what's happening** — the same things that make big apps like Amazon\nand Swiggy feel trustworthy.\n"
}
```

> AGENT

Good — first, notice your **Order IDs are working** (`YFF-20260522-0006`), so that feature deployed fine. ✅

Now, the UPI ID showing up: those orders were placed using the app's **older "manual UPI" payment method** — the one where the buyer pays directly to the farmer's UPI ID (`vinayveeru12@ybl`) and uploads a screenshot, which the farmer manually verifies. That's **completely separate from Razorpay.**

Your app currently offers buyers **three** payment options at checkout:
1. **COD** (cash on delivery)
2. **Manual UPI** ← pay to farmer's UPI ID + screenshot (this is what those orders used)
3. **Razorpay** ← the new automatic online payment (cards + UPI, auto-confirmed)

So those `₹200 strawberry` orders aren't broken — they're just old test orders made with the manual UPI option, which is why they show the UPI ID and "Pay now".

**The real question is a decision for you:** now that Razorpay handles UPI *and* cards automatically (with auto-refunds, receipts, etc.), do you still want the **manual UPI** method? It's the weakest one — it relies on screenshots and manual checking, and it doesn't get the refund/webhook protections we just built.

## To see the change
1. Wait ~1 minute for the Vercel deploy to go **green ✓**.
2. **Important — your app is a PWA, so it caches aggressively.** After the deploy:
   - On phone: fully close the app/tab and reopen, or pull-to-refresh.
   - On desktop: hard-refresh (Ctrl+Shift+R).
   - If it still shows old content, the service worker is serving a cached page — closing all tabs of the site and reopening clears it.

After that, those `📲 UPI — Pay now` / `UPI ID: vinayveeru12@ybl` prompts will be gone, and paid orders will show "✓ Paid online" instead.

## Note on your test data
All those `strawberry ₹200` orders (`0004`–`0013`) are old **pending UPI test orders** — they'll simply show as "Pending" now without the UPI prompt. The `✓ UPI Paid` on `YFF-20260520-0001` is a legacy paid order; it'll just lose the badge. None of this affects real future orders (those are Razorpay/COD only).

If after a clean refresh you *still* see UPI prompts, tell me — that would point to the deploy or cache, and I'll dig in.
